## 2026-10-21 — WITNESS-20261021D idempotent PREPARE retry during a newer PREPARE

- ID WITNESS-20261021D. Scope: off-device witness candidate only. Priority P0 #43 residual, lab candidate, not production authority. Status: local RED then local GREEN; GitHub Actions on the source SHA not yet observed. Asia/Manila 2026-10-21.
- Why: `operate()` in `tools/v060_offdevice_witness_candidate.py` classified PREPARE from the current pending tuple first. A durable older revision therefore lost its idempotent PREPARE retry once a newer PREPARE existed (`pending retry base mismatch`). With no pending row, a floor rolled behind a proven receipt was still classified `ALREADY_FINALIZED_NO_PAID_ACK`. Open draft PR #127 repairs the FINALIZE sibling only; this change does not edit that branch.
- Evidence before the patch, local disposable fixture, no customer data: `sh tests/v060_offdevice_witness_candidate.sh` exited 1 with `WITNESS-0738 FAIL: prepare-replay-during-newer-pending rejected (rc=9): WITNESS-0738 BLOCKED: pending retry base mismatch; reconcile before response (NO PAID ACK)`.
- Repair: proven receipt is consulted first. Retry requires matching nonce, digest and non-empty original `base_digest`, and a floor that is still on that digest or already ahead. Pending state is reported and preserved. Split snapshots are refused.
- Files: `tools/v060_offdevice_witness_candidate.py`, `tests/v060_offdevice_witness_candidate.sh`. Branch `fix/witness-20261021-prepare-during-pending` from `51a4c0cf15446ace04c86cef844a1ce82e09a868`. Source `403d9a69c034ffa1ea4accd6102cc48072f7ee97`.
- After the patch the witness script, `tests/v060_offdevice_witness_reply_auth.py` and `tests/v060_witness_two_stage_recovery.py` exited 0 locally. This ledger sentence does not claim CI. This does not prove independent monotonic hardware, protected P-256 custody, migration, or physical acceptance. `paid_ack_authorized` stays false. VERSION stays 0.5.3.
- NEXT ACTION: push the branch, open a draft PR, and confirm Full CI on the commit that records source `403d9a69c034ffa1ea4accd6102cc48072f7ee97`. Merge only if that run succeeds. If PR #127 merges first, rebase this PREPARE guard onto it without dropping the FINALIZE guard. Do not treat a green software run as production authorization.

- **Priority P0 / issue #32 / status IMPLEMENTED_UNTESTED.** Branch `fix/rent-0723-lease-replay-serialization`, parent verified PAY-0722 dev head `ad21855077773898be22fc5ef4f8170c4b78e189`. No production release, schema, real paid records, signer, or Standalone Rental source changed.
- **Why:** signed duplicate rental coin ACK previously trusted receipt outside global `paid-financial.lock`; fresh rental coin computed lease from a row read before `bp_paid_begin`. An independent admin/controller payment between read and acquisition could therefore have its paid time silently overwritten. This is a race separate from #32 two-file crash atomicity.
- **Code:** `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/rental.sh` `b4cedd31f7cb3aa9a12cbf306c970c53be491e46`: common paid replay lock + unique checked device and receipt snapshots before ACK; after dropping replay lock/acquiring financial writer lock, re-read both again to prevent stale base or duplicate ACK, and re-compute lease from locked fresh record. On concurrent same-event receipt, clear only our own writer pending marker durably and return duplicate without rewriting lease/receipt; on ambiguous storage keep existing halt marker and no ACK. V1 lease and receipt writes are STILL separate.
- **Tests:** new `tests/v060_rental_paid_lock_race.sh` `d9b0e334a7d8d914fa8b2861fb718542e3f6adbb` asserts signed replay no ACK while writer holds lock, independent writer changes paid lease before acquisition and is conserved, another writer claims exact event and retry is idempotent, duplicate/malformed paid rows or receipts fail closed, marker quarantines. Full CI step `dba2462632edd8ec69efc59471dad666267605f6`; P0 path filter `802708fb1414e6d6b5f3f13d0e1b7c2a3423804a` and named P0 execution step `19b58f8c774763631ddca54c3f7dac9250b20b19`.
- **Test evidence:** NONE for this new code yet; former PAY-0722 CI remains parent baseline, not verification. Required: Full exact PR HEAD named RENT-0723, `v060_rental_receipt_midwrite.sh`, `v060_rental_receipt_identity.sh`, `v060_rental_receipt_failure_repro.sh`, paid accounting/replay stress, x86+Android; staged P0 exact SHA. Fix failures without bypassing gates.
- **Limits/rollback:** Not a v2 crash-atomic lease+receipt journal. No signed ACKP1, independent #43 witness, #34 real powercut, owner v1 migration. All P0 issues #31/#32/#34/#43 remain OPEN; `VERSION=0.5.3`, customer install and release blocked. Revert isolated PR to roll back code only, not live balances. **NEXT EXACT ACTION:** open draft PR, monitor named Full and P0 jobs, fix any test/logic regressions, then guarded dev merge only after green exact SHA.

## PAY-0713 — Real member entrypoint synthetic atomic authority bridge — 2026-10-11 Asia/Manila

- **Status: IMPLEMENTED_UNTESTED, P0 #31, no deployment.** Isolated `feat/pay-0713-member-atomic-bridge` from previous verified development HEAD `18f2b52a13f739b0693448d897f6416bf4e9ce87`. Nonproduction synthetic /tmp marker gate and explicit `BP_MEMBER_V2_LAB=1`; normal v1 behavior is unchanged.
- **Root cause:** `bp_member_balance_change` / `bp_member_transfer` currently persist member balances and event receipts separately. A receipt EIO can leave a quarantined changed balance with no committed receipt. Existing native authority fixture is disconnected from installed member entrypoints.
- **Committed source:** `member.sh` `6b1def0029d992a96f5a822cd64e68234bf4f0e6` routes actual function calls only in synthetic mode, bars v1 admin mutations in that mode, and refuses normal live paths; `member-v2-atomic-lab.sh` `89b6d6aa72d3b222919f6d783bcc9c1d07909250` combines member balances, controller/event IDs and result receipts in one versioned checksum-framed authority file and performs a single rename. Derived v1 TSV projections regenerate before retry. Default mode remains v1, no runtime migration or customer paid ACK.
- **Regression source:** `tests/v060_member_v2_atomic_bridge.sh` `84bf6d89d0fe0d301d53aa26c95770d046711d00`; Full workflow `ffcc10870baf21d3993508114992ae5ec7536da5` and staged P0 workflow `625862ee3356c99ce35a9c006072fa02cebd73f6`. Covers before/after authority rename, lost ACK replay, projection failure, transfer sum conservation, receipt ID/source/amount collision, tamper, disabled admin and arbitrary /etc refusal. **No CI conclusion yet; do not label verified.**
- **Critical design limits:** synthetic private marker is required; file hash is NOT HMAC and cannot detect complete older-but-valid snapshot replays; `sync` is not proven durable across target powercut; projection files are not atomic and existing admin/CGI and controller protocol do not yet read v2 authority; explicit v1 seed only on toy test state, NOT an authorized migration. Therefore P0 #31 remains open, #32 rental, #43 antirollback, #34 hardware also open. Do not turn on feature in customer config or claim production 0.6.
- **Rollback:** revert isolated branch; no source default changed and no customer data. **NEXT:** Open draft PR, run exact-head Full and staged P0 CI (with named PAY-0713), diagnose/commit corrections, synchronize all handovers, only then consider guarded dev merge.

## ROLLBACK-0691 — REPRODUCED IN FULL CI AND MERGED INTO DEVELOPMENT, P0 OPEN — 2026-10-10 Asia/Manila

- **Priority P0; REPRODUCED_LAB_ONLY / INTEGRATED_DEVELOPMENT. THIS IS NOT A FIX OR CUSTOMER RELEASE.** The signed v2 native `-DBLAZE_FIXTURE_ONLY` prepaid journal's checksum-valid **older snapshot** can be manually restored after separate member and rental credits have been acknowledged. ## YAML-0685 / ENCRYPTED V1 SEALED-LAB YAML PARSE CORRECTION — COMMITTED_UNTESTED — 2026-10-10 Asia/Manila

- **Scope P1 CI evidence / lab-only; status COMMITTED_UNTESTED:** isolated fix branch `fix/ci-0685-sealed-lab-yaml`, source commit `d83581dd1a831bac704a77dd0d0c03884e00731e`. The distinct [.github/workflows/blazepwifi-v060-encrypted-lab.yml](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/blob/fix/ci-0685-sealed-lab-yaml/.github/workflows/blazepwifi-v060-encrypted-lab.yml) had repeated push failure with **zero jobs** (latest pre-fix [#37959399396](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37959399396)); issue [#33](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/issues/33) tracks diagnosis. ## RNG-0659 + RNG-0660 / VERIFIED FOCUSED CI — 2026-10-09 Asia/Manila — repair broken primary admin auth and reject failed entropy

- **P0 live-source finding:** `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/auth.sh` in source HEAD `9bb9f1b322325bea498d59b7aa8abe64c1a82485` was malformed by an incomplete RNG-0659 text edit: regex `grep -Eq '^[a-f0-9]+` ended in an unterminated quote at line 61, and a complete second copy of almost the entire auth file followed the first CLI `esac`, causing POSIX sh `|| unexpected` at line 286 even after repairing only the first function. This was ACTUAL CI failure, not cosmetic: [fast P0 #37886251548](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37886251548) **FAILURE** at authenticated rental admin bootstrap `auth.sh:508: Unterminated quoted string`; partial repair `acfef9cd69599630fc87826b1c3e241becb2a230` copied known-good standalone R281 CSPRNG block but failed next fast [#37887555144](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37887555144), `auth.sh:286: || unexpected`. Full repair `e57619ce7997ed4432e9429c98d8442b5c8ab98b` truncated duplicate suffix after exactly one complete CLI dispatch; primary library is 11178 bytes, with one random_hex, login and clean_field function each.
- **New security CI trigger:** `.github/workflows/blazepwifi-p0-repro.yml` `d4746053ba304167d731c655914e43d2dc35a5db` includes primary `auth.sh` in source paths and `sh -n` on BOTH R281/full libraries before signed-payment tests. Previously R281 changed triggered test, but full auth source alone did not trigger focused P0.
- **RNG-0660 second-order security issue:** Both full and standalone R281 auth libraries previously ignored failures from `bp_auth_random_hex` when creating an admin password salt (`8` bytes) and admin session/CSRF (`32/24` bytes); could have saved a password with empty salt or issued empty token/CSRF on a platform without a working CSPRNG. Also session append/chmod errors could be falsely ACKed. Source commits full `9484c8275861ff573ed6853345f79e5e074e2dcc`, R281 `86f71899eca8206fb33db95e3f381a4d24454f79` require successful exact-shape lowercase 16/64/48-character cryptographic outputs, refuse token/session or password on entropy shortage, check session TSV append/chmod, and cause `bp_auth_login` to return failure without `login_success` when session creation fails. No signer/private key changed or actual customer admin account rotated.

## MIG-0627 / P0 — IN PROGRESS 2026-10-09: reject/contain uncertain member+rental receipt writes

- **Intent:** eliminate false payment success on receipt EIO and prevent ambiguous retry from silently adding additional money. On current draft `blazepwifi-v0.6.0-audit-foundation`, add shared fail-closed `BP_STATE/paid-state-uncertain` marker/guard to member/rental mutation paths. On a failed receipt write after balance/lease change, best-effort rollback the just-written balance under existing lock, set a persistent halt marker, return failure, and reject further paid changes until an operator performs explicit financial reconciliation. Preflight rental event-log file type before mutation. Existing APIs must not claim an ACK on receipt error. Preserve success behavior on good storage.
- **Affected files:** `openwrt/rootfs/usr/lib/blazepwifi/member.sh`, `rental.sh`, `common.sh` if suitable, synthetic EIO source fixtures, targeted/full CI and canonical handovers. Do not modify production tags/signing/Standalone or disable v0.6 updater guard.
- **Risk:** rollback is not a crash-atomic transaction; if power fails between first write and compensating rollback, ledger can still be uncertain. The durable marker itself can fail on full flash. This mitigation must NOT be interpreted as full resolution of P0 #31/#32 or reason to ship 0.6. A production v2 single-commit journal and target-verified fsync are still mandatory. Reconcile any receipt that might have partly appended before manually clearing halt. Do not reset customer balances automatically.
- **Success:** fixture receipt failure => no success ACK, account/lease restored where possible, unsafe marker raised; any subsequent attempt blocked; normal bank/rental and existing tests pass. New CI exact SHA; rollback by reverting development edits and restoring original tests, no live deployment.
- **NEXT EXACT ACTION:** implement fail-closed error/rollback/marker precisely, test member+rental EIO and quota/replay; update ledger/root/state with actual SHA and CI. Keep release SUCCESS_REPORT=0.

## 2026-10-08 — SYS-0612 P0 OpenWrt firmware upgrade data retention

- **SYS-0612 / P0 / RESEARCH:** OpenWrt sysupgrade only restores specifically preserved config/data, and the BlazePwifi rootfs did not declare a `lib/upgrade/keep.d/` rule for paid-state files. Full firmware sysupgrade therefore risked losing `/etc/blazepwifi/state` and certificate pins despite green *overlay* update tests. Official OpenWrt references: https://openwrt.org/docs/guide-user/installation/generic.sysupgrade and https://openwrt.org/docs/techref/sysupgrade. This is separate from MIG-0610 migration atomicity.
- **SYS-0612 / P0 / CODE COMMITTED; CI PENDING:** New `openwrt/rootfs/lib/upgrade/keep.d/blazepwifi`, commit `9bdd8196d811226ca4feccc963b398b6ade59cee` enumerates only critical `/etc/config/blazepwifi`, `/etc/blazepwifi/state`, `/etc/blazepwifi/portal`, `/etc/uhttpd.crt`, `/etc/uhttpd.key` paths, not `update/snapshots`. Updater whitelist change `e7ac3d55a9c71b92b8e6039287ba3c5a0ba6077f` allows delivery only at `/lib/upgrade/keep.d/blazepwifi`. No customer or operator `/etc/sysupgrade.conf` was modified.
- **SYS-0612 / P0 / TEST COMMITTED; CI PENDING:** `tests/v060_sysupgrade_preservation.sh`, commit `d16ce84af7cd1958ef7a2feb87d983ad8438728d`, checks retention paths, prohibited unbounded backup path, exact whitelist and overlay bundle contents; workflow step commit `3537df27ea66caa2969951c2a510b5bd8fbc4f5c` makes it required. Contract expansion `docs/RELEASE_0.6.0_MIGRATION.md`, commit `d675773097fe51629946ba24fedebd6b86ce6f10`, also identifies same-certificate Android 0.6 rescue needs versionCode greater than 0.6 current, e.g. 60001 over 60000; old 50301 rescue cannot be reused as an in-place update.
- **PRECEDING MIG-0610 MILESTONE / VERIFIED:** Full run [#37776446840](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37776446840) **26 passed, 0 failed**, with candidate gate, and Windows run [#37776446835](https://github.com/BlazingSystems/BlazingSystems-Experiments/actions/runs/37776446835) **2 passed**, exact source SHA `541282869753dc2b64058c45a162ab4b96e83be9`. The later preservation changes are NOT covered by those earlier source gates. `CURRENT_STATE.md` checkpoint `8b91df736cbe09842911e2302e7202d1f2f8014e` records pending work.
- **NEXT EXACT ACTION:** after root/current/ledger synchronization, verify the newly generated run's `validate` preservation regression and full matrix. Require real OpenWrt `sysupgrade -l`/backup size/certificate continuity and off-device encrypted backup on actual router before labeling 0.6.0 firmware production. Keep frozen prior tags intact.

## FWIMG-20261020 — signed compressed image validation guard, 2026-10-20 Asia/Manila

- **Priority/status:** P2 compatibility, `committed_untested_in_GitHub_CI`; source validated with 8 local synthetic tests. Source branch `fix/fwimg-20261020-signed-gzip-guard`; no release authorization.
- **Evidence and root cause:** A supplied x86 OpenWrt EFI `.img.gz` decodes 1,652,850,176 bytes. Python zlib confirms compressed stream CRC; raw `gzip -t` reports `decompression OK, trailing garbage ignored` with nonzero code 2 because the artifact carries an OpenWrt `FWx0` signature record. Appended record framing and 32-bit fwtool CRC validate. Therefore an existing strict `gzip -t` build gate can falsely reject legitimate signed images.
- **Source/test commits:** `221bffa2ef0f2f8d2d5334046ae4296a3fb40b68` (verifier); `acda89f7120d9598a30d349753394944a2f30392` (tests); `b565bbbba83367eb172236c8f7db8940acbb4d53` (x86/SBC build validation); `7ee4d2dbb499ac3821c447b073674739520155c2` (Full CI test registration).
- **Files:** `tools/verify-firmware-gzip.py`; `tests/test_firmware_gzip.py`; `build/build-openwrt-image.sh`; `.github/workflows/blazepwifi-build.yml`; three canonical handovers. Historical shipped releases, Standalone Rental, controller source, money/state and keys unchanged.
- **Tests:** local pre-fix RED (signed/plain valid cases absent), post-fix `python tests/test_firmware_gzip.py` 8/8 PASS; actual user-provided signed-image structure 1,652,850,176 bytes PASS; no simulated or physical payment tests run, no cryptographic signature verification. GitHub Full PR workflow **pending**; no run ID or green status asserted.
- **Risks/rollback:** CRC checks are not authenticity verification; standalone firmware source cannot authorize customer installation, signer identity or compatible hardware. Root build changes limited to replacing two `gzip -t` invocations; revert the isolated PR without modifying customer data. Ruijie reference sysupgrade reports 25.12.0 a non-Blaze OpenWrt derivative and warns DSA/swconfig no-preserve configuration migration; do not flash or reuse blindly.
- **NEXT ACTION:** create draft PR for `fix/fwimg-20261020-signed-gzip-guard`, inspect diff, verify exact-head Full CI including `FWIMG-20261020` regression; if green review for safe development integration. P0 #31/#32/#43/#34 remain OPEN, `VERSION=0.5.3`, no v0.6.0 production release.
